============================================================
COMPUTING METRICS
============================================================

--- RAG-IDB ---
what is the total number of patiemts who had crnry athrscl natve vssl?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Crnry athrscl natve vssl"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['procedures."short_title"', '=', 'crnry athrscl natve vssl']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Crnry athrscl natve vssl"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'crnry athrscl natve vssl']]}
----------

Calculate the number of patients admitted to emergency who had an ld body fluid test by lab
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "EMERGENCY" AND LAB."FLUID" = "LD Body Fluid"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'emergency'], ['lab."fluid"', '=', 'ld body fluid']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "EMERGENCY" AND LAB."LABEL" = "LD, Body Fluid"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'emergency'], ['lab."label"', '=', 'ld, body fluid']]}

----------

count the number of patients who have stayed in the hospital for more than 23 days with a drug phenylephrine hcl.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DAYS_STAY" > "23" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "Phenylephrine Hcl"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."days_stay"', '>', '23'], ['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'phenylephrine hcl']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG_DOSE" FROM PRESCRIPTIONS WHERE PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "Phenytoin"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions."drug_dose"'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'phenytoin']]}
----------

how many patients are diagnosed with hx traumatic fracture?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Hx traumatic fracture"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'hx traumatic fracture']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Hx traumatic fracture"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'hx traumatic fracture']]}

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['lab."fluid"', '=', 'urine'], ['lab."label"', '=', 'magnesium']]}

among patients treated with base drug, how many of them had ventricular tachycardia as primary disease?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS ON DEMOGRAPHIC."HADM_ID" = PRESCRIPTIONS."HADM_ID" 
WHERE PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "BASE DRUG" 
AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "VENTRICULAR TACHYCARDIA"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'ventricular tachycardia'], ['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'base drug']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "VENTRICULAR TACHYCARDIA" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG_TYPE" = "BASE"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'ventricular tachycardia'], ['prescriptions."drug_type"', '=', 'base']]}

Find the number of patients less than 70 years of age who had a percutaneous liver aspiration procedure.
----------
PREDS:

report the number of patients on pr route of drug administration who were diagnosed with antineoplastic and immunosuppressive drugs causing adverse effects in therapeutic use.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE PRESCRIPTIONS."ROUTE" = "PR" 
AND DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Antineoplastic and immunosuppressive drugs causing adverse effects in therapeutic use"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'antineoplastic'], ['immunosuppressive', 'drugs', 'causing adverse effects in therapeutic use'], ['prescriptions."route"', '=', 'pr']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Adv eff antineoplastic" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."ROUTE" = "PR"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'adv eff antineoplastic'], ['prescriptions."route"', '=', 'pr']]}

GOLD: SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_LOCATION" = "EMERGENCY ROOM ADMIT" AND PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Arthrocentesis"
